SDK evidence read so far: CAI_BaseNPC::Spawn gives `additionalequipment` (not "0") only with bits_CAP_USE_WEAPONS (Weapon_Create + Weapon_Equip, weapon named `<npc>_weapon`); Weapon_Equip fills clip/ammo (GetDefaultClip1 vs GetMaxClip1), calls CBaseCombatWeapon::Equip (FollowEntity = bone merge onto the owner, world model), makes it the active weapon; Weapon_CanUse requires every `required` acttable activity (after NPC_TranslateActivity) to have a sequence on the NPC model; animation events 3000-3999 reach the active weapon's Operator_HandleAnimEvent (retail route confirmed by npc-ai-research-20261015a weapon-activity: default handler +408 -> active weapon +598, stunstick table (63->295 SWING, required)); CWeaponPistol NPC fire: EVENT_WEAPON_PISTOL_FIRE -> Weapon_ShootPosition, GetActualShootTrajectory, CSoundEnt combat sound (SOUNDENT_VOLUME_PISTOL, 0.2 s), WeaponSound(SINGLE_NPC), FireBullets(1, ..., VECTOR_CONE_PRECALCULATED, MAX_TRACE_LENGTH, Pistol ammo, tracer every 2), DoMuzzleFlash, clip - 1; NPC cone 5 degrees, bursts 1-3, fire rate 0.5 s, ranges 24-1500. Steps, each unit-tested and reversible:
(1) `hl2-simulation::npc_weapons`: weapon definitions from the installed weapon scripts (scripts/weapon_*.txt: world model, clip sizes, default clips, ammo type, SoundData single_shot_npc/reload_npc/empty) plus the SDK constants above (cones, bursts, rates, ranges, tracer frequency, acttables moved from ai::host); `NpcWeapon` state per actor (clip, next attack, active); equip on map load from `additionalequipment` for classes with bits_CAP_USE_WEAPONS, Weapon_CanUse against the actor's rig (preloaded activities). Read first: GetActualShootTrajectory/GetAttackSpread + proficiency (ai_basenpc.cpp, basecombatweapon_shared.cpp), CAI_ShotRegulator (ai_basenpc.h/cpp), ammodef (hl2_gamerules.cpp: Pistol/SMG1 npc damage skill cvars, tracer types).
(2) Event routing: the actor's animation events with ids 3000-3999 go to its active weapon: stunstick melee (moved from metropolice.rs), pistol/SMG fire through the shared bullet path the player uses (traces, impacts/decals, TraceBleed, damage to player/NPCs/props with npc damage from skill.cfg, positional NPC weapon sounds), clip decrement, empty -> reload (ACT_RELOAD via acttable). No weapon: events are dropped.
(3) Ranged combat for weapon-carrying cops (and later other classes): COND_CAN_RANGE_ATTACK1 (weapon range, LOS, aim cone: WeaponRangeAttack1Condition), SCHED_RANGE_ATTACK1/TASK_RANGE_ATTACK1 with the shot regulator bursts, npc_metropolice's own ranged schedules (burst attack, SelectRangeAttackSchedule). Read first: npc_metropolice.cpp SelectCombatSchedule/RangeAttack paths.
(4) Host: per actor, the weapon world model loaded at map load (setup-time) and drawn bone-merged to the NPC skeleton (matching bone names, EF_BONEMERGE); stunstick on/off visual (C_WeaponStunStick glow/beam sprites); muzzle flash and tracers reuse the arsenal effects. F4 overlay (owner, session 18: the list does not scroll into view; it must be usable for NPC battles): two columns, a scrolling class list that keeps the selection visible and beside it the weapons that class can use (bits_CAP_USE_WEAPONS + Weapon_CanUse; none for e.g. zombies), the chosen weapon equipped through Weapon_Equip; per-spawn allegiance (friend or foe: the spawned NPC's disposition toward the player and the opposite side, like ai_relationship) and a visible notarget toggle (NPCs ignore the player and act only on the world and each other), replacing the cloud's global I/O/P hotkeys as menu rows. NPC-vs-NPC battles additionally need AI for more classes (npc_combine_s, npc_citizen; DESIGN 14 step 5): a separate step after this one.
(5) Later with DESIGN 14r: dropped weapons on death (Weapon_Drop as a runtime weapon entity the player can pick up).
Validate: unit tests per step (equip/capability/CanUse, event routing incl. unarmed, spread cone statistics, clip/reload, burst timing); packaged: F4 cop with weapon_pistol hating the player (stance P) vs native `npc_create_equipment weapon_pistol` + `npc_create` with notarget off (shot cadence, damage per hit from the HUD, sounds, tracers); d1_canals_01 pistol cops; stunstick visual vs native T065205Z frames. Not tested until then.

14s plan (session 19, owner report: NPCs float: barrelpush cop, maker_passerby #442, citizen_queue maker #517). Cause found: the floating NPCs are npc_template_maker templates (spawnflag SF_NPC_TEMPLATE 2048: citizen_passerby #439 z 104, citizen_queue #518 z 132 on d1_trainstation_02; barrelpush_cop1 #126 z 288 and 18 more cops/scanners on d1_canals_01). SDK MapEntity_ParseAllEntities stores a template's keyvalues and removes it (IsTemplate); only CTemplateNPCMaker spawns copies. We spawned them in place. SDK evidence: monstermaker.cpp (CBaseNPCMaker Spawn/MakerThink/CanMakeNPC/HumanHullFits/IsDepleted/Enable/Disable/Toggle/inputs/DeathNotice from CBaseCombatCharacter::UpdateOnRemove; CTemplateNPCMaker MakeNPC/FindSpawnDestination (CriterionVisibility 0 yes/1 no/2 don't care, CriterionDistance 0 nearest/1 farthest/2 random of five HumanHullFits tries)/MakeNPCInRadius/PlaceNPCInRadius = FindSpotForNPCInRadius; flags START_ON 1, NPCCLIP 8, FADE 16, INF_CHILD 32, NO_DROP 64, HIDEFROMPLAYER 128, ALWAYSUSERADIUS 256), CNPCSpawnDestination ReuseDelay/RenameNPC/OnSpawnNPC, CAI_BaseNPC::StartNPC (first think: unless MOVETYPE_FLY/FLYGRAVITY, bits_CAP_MOVE_FLY, SF_NPC_FALL_TO_GROUND 4, IsWaitingToRappel or a move parent, FloorPoint(origin + 0.1, MASK_NPCSOLID, 0, -2048): a zero-height "pizzabox" of the hull from z + MOVE_HEIGHT_EPSILON 0.0625, one start-solid retry, failure keeps origin + 0.1; else SetGroundEntity(NULL)), PhysicsStepRunTimestep (MOVETYPE_STEP gravity: half gravity before and after the move while not on ground, sv_gravity 600). Retail corroboration: npc-ai-research-20261015b spawn-floor (the -2048 floor range, bypass predicates, maker NO_DROP handling) and 20261015c (npc_metropolice overrides only IsWaitingToRappel). Steps, unit-tested and reversible:
(1) Scene holds SF_NPC_TEMPLATE NPCs (killed, unnamed for inputs) like point_template children.
(2) `npc_makers`: npc_template_maker state and inputs (Spawn, Enable, Disable, Toggle, SetMaxChildren, AddMaxChildren, SetMaxLiveChildren, SetSpawnFrequency, SpawnNPCInRadius, SpawnMultiple, ChangeDestinationGroup, SetMinimumSpawnDistance), MakerThink every SpawnFrequency, CanMakeNPC (live limit, 34-unit box of NPCs/player with the HumanHullFits fallback, HIDEFROMPLAYER view cone 0.766 + LOS), destinations and radius placement, OnSpawnNPC (activator = the child), OnAllSpawned, DeathNotice -> OnAllLiveChildrenDead/OnAllSpawnedDead. The first spawn activates the held template entity itself; later copies need DESIGN 14r and are counted as unsupported until then.
(3) StartNPC once per NPC on its first live tick (map, maker, F4 pool) for classes with a known hull (`physics::npc_hull`); SF_NPC_FALL_TO_GROUND NPCs (and radius-placed maker children) fall under step gravity until they land. AI's CheckOnGround/SCHED_FALL_TO_GROUND for NPCs pushed off ledges is later.
Validate: unit tests (template hold, maker limits/criteria/depletion/outputs, FloorPoint on a synthetic floor incl. start-solid and failure, falling); owned-map test (d1_trainstation_02 templates held, makers found); packaged d1_trainstation_02 plaza and d1_canals_01 barrelpush trigger vs native (the cop appears on the floor where native shows it).

14r plan: runtime entity creation (owner decision 2026-10-12; replaces the F4 spawn pool). Source creates entities at any time: CC_NPC_Create (ai_concommands.cpp) saves IsPrecacheAllowed, SetAllowPrecache(true), CreateEntityByName, Precache (late precache loads the model then, a hitch in native), DispatchSpawn, trace placement, Teleport, Activate, then restores the precache flag; the engine entity list is dynamic (2048 edicts). Our fixed list (Arc<World>, Scene.states/physics/AI indexed by load order, Bevy meshes built once per map) was a convenience for 1:1 map reconstruction, not a principle. Bounded steps, each reversible and unit-tested with synthetic worlds:
(1) Entity store: spawned entities append to a growable list with stable ids (never reused; removal marks killed, as map kills do). Either `World` becomes growable behind the host's ownership (no Arc sharing in frame systems) or spawned entities live in a `Scene`-owned extension table addressed by the same id space (ids >= world.entities.len()); choose after measuring how many systems clone the Arc.
(2) `Scene::spawn_entity(properties) -> id`: pushes a State built by the same code path as map entities (Scene::new's per-entity setup factored into one function) and queues Spawn/Activate-equivalent work (outputs, parenting, defaults).
(3) Simulation tables: Physics::add_entity_colliders(id, instance) (NPC_GROUP for npc_*), npc Controller actor registration, AiRuntime::add_actor (class behavior from the class name), LookTargets/eyes registration.
(4) Late precache: a class's model, clips, collision, materials and sounds load on an asynchronous worker (the map-load pattern) the first time it is spawned; the request is queued with its placement and inserted when the assets are ready (no blocking reads in frame systems; a frame or more of delay instead of native's hitch). Cache per map; release with MapOwned resources on map change.
(5) Bevy host: build one entity's meshes/skeleton/material instances on insertion (factor the per-instance part of the map build), plus despawn on kill.
(6) Remove the F4 pool (`ai::spawn::append_pool`, POOL_KEY) once (1)-(5) pass; keep the placement and console parsing. Then npc_maker/npc_template_maker, manhack launch, zombie headcrab release and dropped weapons can use the same path.
Progress (cloud 2026-10-12): step (1) chose the Scene-owned extension table: `Scene::spawned` (ids from world.entities.len() upward, never reused), `Scene::entity(world, id)` lookup, and `Scene::spawn_entity(world, entity) -> id` using the factored `Scene::initial_state` (the same code as map entities). Not called by the host yet. Audit before calling it: hl2-simulation has 42 direct `world.entities[...]` index sites (entities.rs, actors.rs, monitors.rs, ai/runtime.rs, ai/spawn.rs) and hl2-bevy 7 (eyes.rs, assets.rs, rendering.rs, main.rs, monitors.rs); any of them reached with a spawned id would panic, and 7 loops walk every state. Step (2) must route those through `Scene::entity` (or skip ids >= world.entities.len() where only map entities apply) before the first runtime spawn. Step (2) part 1 done (9b296ea and the next commit): entities.rs's 23 runtime sites use `Scene::ent` (spawned entities are leaked `&'static Entity`, so lookups borrow only the World); ai/runtime.rs sentence speaker and hl2-bevy eyes.rs tracking use `Scene::entity`; the rest are load-time loops over map entities or map-only classes (monitors, cameras, actors.rs instance registration, rendering's per-map build) and stay as they are. A unit test ticks a spawned cop through the Scene and physics pose loops and kills it by input. Remaining for step (2): `world.entities.get(id)` sites return None for spawned ids (no panic, but the entity is skipped): review them per system while doing steps (3)-(5). Step (3) started: `AiRuntime::add_actor(id, entity)` (shares `actor_for` with map loading) gives a spawned npc_metropolice its AI actor (test: a runtime-spawned cop thinks and keeps its baton). `Physics::add_entity_hull(id, mins, maxs, origin)` gives a spawned NPC a class-hull box collider (CONTENTS_MONSTER, moved by set_entity like map NPC colliders; test: blocks a trace at the hull face and follows the state); the model's own collision pieces replace it after the late precache (4). Still to do in (3): the npc Controller ground-actor registration (actors.rs, needs the class's rig from the late precache).
Validate: unit tests per step; local: F4 spawn of a not-yet-loaded class (time to appear, no frame spikes in --report timing), 50 spawns, map change after spawning, regression batch, native comparison of npc_create_aimed placement.
14r session 25 plan update (owner 2026-10-21: runtime spawning is the next milestone; speed it up): split by what must load. (A) Classes whose model, clips, locomotion variants, weapons and sounds are already loaded on the map (every F4 class today, through the pool) spawn as new entities: Scene::spawn_entity -> AiRuntime::add_actor -> Physics::add_entity_hull -> Controller registration from per-model locomotion variants cached at map load (no VFS in frame systems) -> the Bevy host builds one model instance from World::model_assets (factor the per-instance `append` of spawn_map: meshes, skeleton/skinning, materials, NPC weapon models) and despawns on kill/map change. This removes the 3-per-class limit for every pooled class without any new asset loading. (B) Late precache for classes/models the map never loaded (the async worker pattern of map loads), then npc_maker/template repeat spawns, prop_physics_create/ent_create, gibs as entities and dropped weapons. (C) Remove the pool. Validate: unit tests per step (spawned ids, AI/physics/controller tables), packaged: 20 F4 soldiers in the hall (frame timing in --report), kill/despawn, map change after spawning, regression batch.
14r part A status (session 26, 2026-10-21, `wip/runtime-entities-20261021`): done and packaged. Step (1) was re-decided after measuring: the host's `Gameplay.world` is the only long-lived `Arc<World>` (the load's `LoadedMap` copy is dropped by install_map; frame systems take short-lived clones), so a spawn grows the map's World in place with `Arc::make_mut` (no copy; `world_copies` in the report counts the copies a held clone would force, 0 in every run) instead of the cloud's Scene-side extension table, which is removed. Every `world.entities[id]` / model-instance lookup then works for spawned ids unchanged. The F4 pool entities stay as hidden templates (they make the map load every asset of the class, like a precache) and are never shown; `ai::spawn::instantiate` copies a template's keyvalues (minus the pool key, with the new origin/angles) and model instance; `Scene::spawn_entity` appends entity + state; `Physics::add_spawned_instance` runs the same per-instance code as map load (`add_model_instance`, factored out of Physics::new: hull box, studio hitboxes, contents); `AiRuntime::add_actor` (also invalidates the NPC sight-candidate list, which had been built once on first use: spawned NPCs were never seen by others); `Controller::copy_actor` (hull, locomotion variants, eye offset); the host's `rendering::spawn_runtime_draws` clones the template's draws next frame (GPU-skinned static meshes shared, flex/eye/CPU-skinned meshes and lit materials copied per entity, a new joint hierarchy, NPC weapon models bone-merged onto it) and `Eyes::copy_actor`. Templates cycle per overlay entry (citizen templates carry different heads). Limit: Source's MAX_EDICT_BITS 11 is compiled into the engine and its network entity handles (2048 edicts; npc_create past it fails in ED_Alloc); ours is `ai::spawn::MAX_EDICTS` (2048 live runtime spawns by default, `--max-edicts N`, 0 = none). Owner question (2026-10-21) on raising it for future netcode/mods: only Valve wire compatibility needs 11 bits; a future HL2-RS protocol should carry entity ids as 32-bit/varint with a generation and announce the server's limit at connect, and modkit-core contracts should address entities by id, never by a fixed table size. Validation: unit tests (template copy + physics hull/hitbox at the new place; a foe spawned after the first think is seen); packaged station hall: 20 soldiers (artifacts/s26/twenty.png; fixed step 4-6 ms, physics ~3 ms), 10 foe soldiers vs 10 friend rebels all runtime-spawned (two refused with Bad Position at the wall, like native; 488 shots, 16 deaths; step 4-6 ms, peak 14.6 ms while 18 fight, physics 2-3 ms; artifacts/s26/battle-cap.png), map change to d1_trainstation_03 after spawning 5 (loads, 60 actors = the map's). Not done: Bevy draws of killed runtime NPCs are hidden, not despawned (like map NPCs; memory grows by one model instance per spawn until the map changes); render frame timing beyond the fixed step not measured; native comparison of large spawn counts not done. npc_template_maker repeat spawns (part B item) are done too: the maker queues a CopyRequest (SF_NPC_TEMPLATE removed, fall flag per the maker) that the host creates through the same path and the maker adopts (OnSpawnNPC with the child, live children, MaxNPCCount); packaged on d1_canals_01 (barrelpush_cop3_maker). Next: part B (late precache of classes the map never loaded on an async worker; prop_physics_create/ent_create; gibs and dropped weapons as entities), then part C (the pool becomes a template cache only).
14r part B status (session 26, same branch): (1) Late precache for physics props: `runtime_props` (hl2-bevy) runs `assets::prepare_model` on a worker thread for a model the map never loaded (a one-entity World through the map's own `models::append_models`, `append_break_physics`, `append_rigs`, `read_hull` (studiohdr hull_min/max at 104/116), the effects' gib models, `load_material` for materials not yet known), with the map's mounted VFS kept in `LoadedMap::vfs`; the host merges the tables (`Gameplay::merge_model`, keys it lacks only), uploads new textures into `rendering::ModelCache` (the map's texture handles by VTF path and pixel-free material definitions, kept by spawn_map), adds gib meshes (`Effects::add_models`), places the prop like SDK CreatePhysicsProp and builds its draws with `rendering::build_model_draws` (per-material meshes, per-entity lit materials, GPU skeleton when weighted). `Gameplay::insert_entity` is the shared creation path (templates, props, drops). F4 lists 28 physics props after the NPC classes. (2) Dropped NPC weapons (CBaseCombatCharacter::Event_Killed -> Weapon_Drop): see STATUS session 26. Not done in part B: ent_create/prop_physics_create console routing (hl2-ui console parser: owner OK pending), NPC classes outside the F4 list (late precache of NPC rigs/locomotion), gibs as entities. Part C unchanged.

14r part B status (session 28, owner request 2026-10-21: the F4 Props tab was hard to navigate): plan and result in one bounded step, all in the existing overlay (`ai::spawn::Overlay`, host `movement::controls`, `spawn_panel`). (1) The Props tab opens on a "* common" folder: the installed subset of `SPAWN_PROPS` (explosive barrel, wooden crates, ...) with plain names (`SPAWN_PROP_NAMES`), shown next to the model path. (2) Type-to-filter: "/" (typed character, any layout) or Ctrl+F or a click on the filter box starts typing; the filter is a lowercase substring of the model path or the common name; the first folder lists all matches, then each folder with matches; Enter ends typing, Backspace (repeats) edits; while typing, movement and action keys are ignored. (3) Mouse: while F4 is open the cursor is free (holding the right button locks it again and looks around; the cursor relocks when F4 closes); a click selects a tab, folder, model, class or option, a click on the selected item acts like Enter (spawn / open the folder), option toggles act on every click, "more above/below" notes page; the wheel moves the selection of the column under the pointer, 3 rows per notch; mouse buttons do not fire weapons while F4 is open. (4) Held Up/Down/PageUp/PageDown repeat after 0.35 s every 0.04 s. (5) Lists fill the window (28 rows at 720 p, from the font metrics). (6) Escape stops typing, else closes the overlay, before pause. (7) A small marker shows the screen center (the aim) over the panel. Script actions: `spawn_overlay_text`, `spawn_overlay_click` (target), `spawn_overlay_scroll` (column, rows); `spawn_overlay` keys gained `filter` and `backspace`. Not ours to change: the hl2-ui menus (@OrbitalMan). Session 26/27 prop fixtures that count folders now start one folder later (the common folder is first).
14n session 29 plan and status (owner playtest notes 2026-10-22; branch `wip/squads-20261022`). Bounded steps, each reversible: (1) COLLISION_GROUP_INTERACTIVE_DEBRIS: SF_PHYSPROP_DEBRIS props (the can cop's PopCan01a has spawnflags 261) collide only with the world and ordinary props (CGameRules::ShouldCollide), so a thrown can passed through the cop; CPlayerPickupController::Init and Physgun_OnPhysGunPickup (pickup, hold, punt) switch debris to interactive debris (everything but debris, interactive debris and the player), and CBaseEntity::VPhysicsUpdate turns it back when the body sleeps (`Physics::make_interactive_debris`, GROUP_7). Done; packaged: the thrown can hits cupcop (`thrown_object_hits` 1, OnCupCopped -> achievement_can_hitcop) and enters throwcan_trigger -> cupcop_fail_relay (Failure scene, knock-out), the map's own branch. (2) CPhysicsProp/CPhysBox pickup outputs: OnPhysGunPickup, OnPhysGunOnlyPickup (cannon), OnPhysGunPunt, OnPlayerPickup (+USE or cannon), OnPhysGunDrop; the can's OnPhysGunPickup -> cupcop_can_pickup -> lcs_CupCop_nag_putin ("put it in the trash") never fired before. Done; packaged: NPC_MetroPolice.Cupcop.PutItInTheTrash after the pickup. (3) Player-ally speech requests carried an empty actor model, so `$gender` waves picked from both genders (GenderExpandString needs the model): the speaker's model is passed. Done (unit-level; owner listening). (4) Gravity gun: SecondaryAttack calls DoEffect(EFFECT_HOLDING) on every pickup attempt, found or not, until ItemPostFrame's DoEffect(EFFECT_READY) on release, and CanPickupObject(NULL) fails, so the deny sound (SPECIAL3, Weapon_PhysCannon.TooHeavy) plays once per press wherever the player aims. Done (unit tests; the motor hold loop still plays only on AttachObject as in the SDK: owner to confirm which sound native plays). (5) Studio bodygroups: `ModelInstance.body` (m_nBody; asset keys gain `@body` only when nonzero, so every existing cache key is unchanged), `read_model_body` selects each body part's submodel ((body / base) % models) from raw mstudiomodel_t (148 bytes) / mstudiomesh_t (116 bytes) records because vmdl 0.2 steps later submodels with a wrong stride (the zombie headcrab came out empty); `entity_body`: the `body` keyvalue, then zombie Spawn bodygroups (ZOMBIE_BODYGROUP_HEADCRAB 1 on npc_zombie/_torso/fastzombie/poisonzombie; the poison zombie's nest crabs 2-4 from crabcount, all three by default; one or two crabs pick their mask by entity index instead of RandomInt). Done; packaged capture artifacts/s29/zombie-look.png (classic, fast and poison zombies with their crabs). (6) npc_zombie AI (`ai::zombie`, SDK npc_basezombie.cpp/npc_zombie.cpp): AI ground mover walking for ACT_RUN (CZombie::NPC_TranslateActivity; the classic model has no ACT_RUN, so its walk serves both styles; the root-motion validator now ignores the walks' 1e-4 sideways drift), FOV 0.2, base sound interests, CNPC_BaseZombie::MaxYawSpeed, schedules SCHED_ZOMBIE_CHASE_ENEMY (non-episodic), _MELEE_ATTACK1, _POST_MELEE_WAIT, _WANDER_MEDIUM/_STANDOFF/_FAIL (TASK_WANDER 48-384 units), MeleeAttack1Conditions (55-unit reach origin to origin, facing 0.7, the hull cube swept along the facing meets the enemy), claw events AE_ZOMBIE_ATTACK_RIGHT/LEFT/BOTH (ClawAttack: no swing through a wall to the enemy; CheckTraceHullAttack 55 with the 13-unit cube; sk_zombie_dmg_one_slash; the SDK view and velocity punches; Zombie.AttackHit/Miss), Zombie.Attack/Footstep*/Scuff*/Alert (TASK_SOUND_WAKE and AE_ZOMBIE_ALERTSOUND)/Pain/Die, idle moans every U(2, 5) s (CZombie::PrescheduleThink; gagged zombies are quiet); `ai::zombie::SOUNDS` are preloaded (a cue that is not preloaded is dropped: the first packaged run was silent). Every npc_* model now loads `NPC_BASE_AI_ACTIVITIES` (idle, walk/run, turns, attacks, flinches, deaths, jump/leap/climb) in addition to class lists (owner request: groundwork for later classes). Done; packaged hall (artifacts/s29/zombie-hall): the zombie walks ~170 units to the walking player and claws 9 times (all hits) in 19 s. Not done: headcrab release on death (ReleaseHeadcrab: a live npc_headcrab or a ragdoll crab), torso split, physics swats, door bashing, burning, the moan envelope, fast/poison/torso classes, headcrabs; native comparison not done. (7) Cop chatter (owner report: native trainstation cops are silent unless approached): CAI_BaseNPC GatherConditions samples, and rolls IdleSound, only with COND_IN_PVS (a player in the NPC's PVS), ShouldAlwaysThink or in combat, once per decision; UpdateEfficiency sets the decision interval (0.1-0.6 s) from state, PVS, player facing and distance (`ai::efficiency`, PC floor AIE_NORMAL). The host's attention PVS set (`attention_pvs`, BSP PVS of the head from the player eye) now gates the idle roll. Native evidence (oracle dwell 60 s at the same plaza spot, noclip + notarget, `developer 1; snd_showstart 1`; private work/hl2-decompiled/cop-chatter-20261022): one cop sentence (!METROPOLICE_IDLE0, 90 dB, vol 0.8) in the whole run, no question/answer pairs; ours after the gate: 6 lines (10 before). Retail server.dll CNPC_MetroPolice::IdleSound (FUN_1039eb50) and CAI_SentenceBase::Speak (FUN_100d22c0) match the SDK, so the remaining rate gap is in the base think/gather path (RunAI decision skipping, think rebalancing, or something not yet found): open, next is the retail CAI_BaseNPC::GatherConditions/NPCThink decompile and a native dwell without notarget near a lone cop. (8) Burning props (owner report): `fire::Fires` ports CBreakableProp::OnTakeDamage's fire rules (non-deadly DMG_BLAST/DMG_BURN ignite flammable props; bullets bring PROPINTER_FIRE_IGNITE_HALFHEALTH props to half health, refill them and ignite them for U(10, 15) s, a bullet on a burning one explodes it; deadly blasts on PROPINTER_FIRE_EXPLOSIVE_RESIST props leave health for up to 5 s of burning, 0.1-0.2 s within 32 units) and CEntityFlame (first think 0.1 s, then 0.2 s; 1 HP direct DMG_BURN|DMG_DIRECT to its prop; 4 HP radius DMG_BURN with linear falloff within size / 2 to the player, props and NPCs; flames from other fires are ignored while burning; General.BurningObject loop, General.StopBurning). PropData carries fire_interactions (the model sections beside prop_data, or a base's own); owned: oildrum001_explosive flammable + explosive_resist + ignite halfhealth (health 20), gascan001a flammable only, wood_pallet001a and oildrum001 none. Packaged (artifacts/s29/barrel-burn): the second pistol shot ignites the runtime explosive barrel (2.85 s), it burns ~3 s and explodes (5.89 s). Not done: the flame visuals: C_EntityFlame plays the "burning_character" particle system (particles/burning_fx.pcf; four child systems, 29 operator kinds, the animated sheet material particle/fire_burning_character), burning NPCs (zombies), EF_DIMLIGHT, water. (9) `source_assets::pcf`: binary DMX 2 reader and DmeParticleSystemDefinition model (all nine installed manifest files parse; owned test checks burning_character). The particle runtime (emitters, initializers, operators, render_animated_sprites with sheet sequences) is the next bounded step; the operator inventory is in private work/hl2-decompiled/pcf-20261022/operators.txt (81 kinds across the files).

14n session 31 plan (performance report, @OrbitalMan 2026-10-22: FPS ~4 while the can cop chases a player he cannot reach at the street ladder; branch `wip/session31-20261022`). Evidence: owned measurement (`assets::owned_route_cost`, routes from the can cop's post to all 386 d1_trainstation_02 nodes): a failed route costs ~1.9 s (1002 link probes); successful long routes 0.3-1.6 s. Cause: our A* validated every expanded graph link with a stepped hull `ground_move` (~1.9 ms per link). Nodes south of the street (-3744..-3616, -5264..-5088) are not connected to the cop's component, so each failed chase search swept the whole component; the chase loop re-requests about 10 times a second. SDK: CAI_Pathfinder::IsLinkUsable checks only link flags (bits_LINK_OFF and dynamic-link allow lists), the hull's accepted move types and IsUnusableNode. The only trace-based check is IsLinkStillStale/CheckStaleRoute, for links an NPC flagged as stale (CAI_Navigator::MarkCurWaypointFailedLink when a move along the link fails: stale for 4 s, then rechecked at most once per think, 1 s more when still blocked). Bounded steps: (1) search costs are graph distances; links marked stale (our blocked advance between two route nodes) are unusable until expiry, then rechecked with the connector; the route's door segments are probed lazily (current and next segment) instead of all at plan time; (2) CAI_BaseNPC RememberUnreachable/IsUnreachable (3 s, 120-unit tolerance) on TASK_GET_PATH_TO_ENEMY/_LKP (and the chase task that chains them) failures, COND_ENEMY_UNREACHABLE in GatherEnemyConditions, BestEnemy reachable-before-unreachable, elusion of an unreachable unseen enemy whose LKP is not visible, ForceChooseNewEnemy; (3) the chase task chains PATH_TO_ENEMY (enemy within 300 of its LKP) or PATH_TO_ENEMY_LKP like the SDK. Validation: unit tests (stale links, unreachable expiry/tolerance, task failure without a path request), the owned route-cost measurement before/after, packaged can-cop catwalk run (path stats in the report: `npc_paths`), regression batch (NPC routes on campaign maps can change: inspect).

14n session 31 particles (DESIGN 14n session 30 (8) next step, done for five operators): the retail operator semantics come from the SDK's compiled particles.lib (x64 objects with symbols, linked into a private analysis DLL; notes in private work/hl2-decompiled/particles-20261022). Ported to `pcf_runtime`: C_OP_InterpolateRadius, C_OP_FadeIn, C_OP_FadeOut (with the DmxElementUnpack defaults), C_OP_ColorInterpolate and C_OP_BasicMovement drag. Next, in the same way: C_INIT_CreateOnModel, C_OP_LockToBone, C_INIT_InitialVelocityNoise/CreationNoise/AgeNoise/PositionWarp, C_OP_RandomForce, the continuous emitter and the sprite renderer's dual sequences.

14n session 31 ragdolls (DESIGN 14n session 30 (11) steps (b)-(c), done): `source_assets::phy::ragdoll_model` binds every PHY ragdoll solid to the studio bone of the same name. Its convex pieces stay in bone space (owned test: placed at the bones' reference matrices, every solid sits on its bone, gaps 0.06-3.8 units on police/combine_soldier/male_07/zombie). Each constraint carries the child-in-parent reference transform (Studio_CalcBoneToBoneTransform), and the result is loaded with each NPC rig as `World::model_ragdolls` (a modkit-core contract). `Physics::spawn_ragdoll` (RagdollCreate) creates one body per solid at the bone's current world matrix with the authored mass, damping and IVP inertia. Bodies are joined by Rapier generic joints: locked translation, per-axis angular limits about the child bone's axes, friction motors, no contacts between joined pairs. Collision rules (selfcollisions/collisionpair) go through the contact pair filter. Client ragdoll behavior: a ragdoll group collides with the world and props, props are not pushed (dominance -1), and player/NPC/debris collisions and every server query (traces, use, gravity gun, movement) ignore it. `ragdoll_nudge` applies the death force like RagdollCreate: all of it at the hit solid's center, plus a mass share at that point on every other solid. Death forces: the bullet/club impulse of the killing hit; blasts use CalculateExplosiveDamageForce (damage x 300, clamp 30000; the random scale is omitted). `hl2_simulation::ragdolls` spawns from the death pose (actor_matrices are skinning matrices: pose = skin x bind), keeps unsimulated bones at their death-pose locals, writes model-space skinning matrices into `Scene::ragdoll_matrices` (which `actor_matrices` returns), moves the entity origin to the root solid (render placement and bounds) and keeps at most 8 (g_ragdoll_maxcount). Rendering draws ragdolled NPCs and their eyes but not their bone-merged weapons. Not done: animation velocity at death (RagdollApplyAnimationAsVelocity), the force bone from the hit bone (nearest solid instead), rotInertiaLimit, animated friction, fade, prop_ragdoll map entities (step (d)), headcrab release, burning ragdolls, server ragdolls, native A/B of fall/limits.

14n session 32 ragdoll playtest fixes (owner playtest of session 31, 2026-10-26; branch `wip/session32-20261026`). Evidence: SDK client/fx_impact.cpp (FX_AffectRagdolls, CRagdollEnumerator), client/c_te_explosion.cpp (CRagdollExplosionEnumerator), client/c_baseanimating.cpp (C_ClientRagdoll::ImpactTrace), client/c_stickybolt.cpp (StickRagdollNow, CRagdollBoltEnumerator), server/hl2/weapon_crossbow.cpp (BoltTouch), shared/baseentity_shared.cpp (FireBullets, RagdollImpact), shared/takedamageinfo.cpp (AddMultiDamage), shared/ragdoll_shared.cpp (RagdollCreate, LRU); retail client.dll (private work/hl2-decompiled/ragdolls-20261026): C_ClientRagdoll::ImpactTrace FUN_100a5510 (shots 4000, blasts **500**, not the SDK's 4000), FX_AffectRagdolls FUN_1012ffa0 (skips while more than one ragdoll simulates), CRagdollBoltEnumerator::EnumElement FUN_1023cf20 (as the SDK), CRagdollLRURetirement::Update FUN_101aa3a0 (the SDK's two algorithms chosen at run time by hl2_episodic). Bounded steps: (1) the player's convex SAT hull path skipped only listed collision groups, not the ragdoll group (ragdolls blocked the player): add it; unit test sweeps the player hull through a ragdoll. (2) Death forces: FireBullets multidamage (pellets on one target add up and apply when a pellet hits another target or at the end; later pellets on a corpse killed by the same shot add their force); the force solid is m_nForceBone (the last trace attack's solid, the root for blasts without one); blasts add CalculateExplosiveDamageForce's and CalcDamageForceVector's fudges; crossbow kills carry CalculateMeleeDamageForce x 0.7. (3) Client ragdoll effects queued per tick (`Scene::ragdoll_impacts`): every bullet/club trace (FX_AffectRagdolls: the first ragdoll on the shot, 4000 on the root solid at start + fraction x (hit - start), skipped for ragdolls created this tick and while more than one ragdoll simulates), explosion temp entities (each ragdoll within the radius in line of sight: (dir x (magnitude - magnitude/radius x dist) + 32 up) x 500 at the root's center), BoltImpact (the first ragdoll solid within 128 units in front of the bolt's wall point: mass x 2, ball-socket to the world at the wall point, Weapon_Crossbow.BoltSkewer). Ragdolls stay out of every server trace and projectile sweep (no meat shields). Validation: unit tests (hull sweep, ragdoll ray/impulse/pin, multidamage force), packaged hall fixtures (artifacts/s32: walk-through, pistol/shotgun kills, corpse shots/frag, crossbow kill at a wall and into a corpse, lined-up citizens), native A/B of pistol and shotgun deaths (oracle npc_create + kill, captures at fixed delays). Also done: CAI_BaseNPC::PassesDamageFilter friendly immunity (F4 debug option) and the ragdoll fade (`Ragdolls::retire`: retail LRU non-episodic branch, frustum-only out-of-view test, 600 alpha/s; host material blend). Not in this step: prop_ragdoll, headcrab release, burning ragdolls, friendly immunity for projectiles/blasts.

14n session 33 friendly immunity for projectiles and blasts (owner request 2026-10-26; branch `wip/session33-20261027`). Evidence: SDK CAI_BaseNPC::PassesDamageFilter, CBaseGrenade::Explode (m_hThrower), CGrenadeAR2 (GetThrower), CMissile::DoExplosion and CCrossbowBolt::BoltTouch (GetOwnerEntity), CPropCombineBall::OnHitEntity (bShouldHit: the target's IRelationType(owner) != D_LI), CBreakableProp::OnTakeDamage/Break (m_hLastAttacker, else the physics attacker within 2 s: "player allies get immunity from barrels exploded by the player"), CBreakableProp::OnPhysGunPickup/OnPhysGunDrop (SetPhysicsAttacker), CBaseCombatCharacter::VPhysicsShadowCollision (HasPhysicsAttacker(1.0) names the player), CEntityFlame/env_physexplosion/trigger_hurt/InputSetHealth (the entity itself: passes). Retail server.dll (private work/hl2-decompiled/friendly-projectiles-20261027): PassesDamageFilter FUN_10057d20, VPhysicsShadowCollision FUN_100f58e0 (1.0), CBreakableProp::Break FUN_1021ce50 (2.0): as the SDK. Bounded steps: (1) `projectiles::Attacker` (none, player, entity) on every `Damage`; `gameplay::damage_refused` generalizes the session 32 rule to any attacker (player: the default table and the F4 debug option; an NPC: its default-table disposition toward the target; dead or non-NPC attackers pass); the entity damage path refuses before health/outputs (OnDamagedByPlayer/OnDamagedByPlayerSquad for the player). (2) Projectiles carry their owner (`Projectile::owner` replaces `npc_owner`): the player's launches, NPC grenade tosses/balls/missiles; bolts, ball dissolves and `grenade_explosion` name it. (3) Explosive props: the last NPC/player attacker per prop, else the player when a physgun/+USE pickup or drop was within 2 s, carried by `prop_explosions`. (4) Thrown props: NPC impact damage names the player within 1 s of that influence; the gravity-gun punt names the player. Validation: unit tests (refusal per attacker, NPC grenade on a citizen, barrel attacker), packaged hall fixtures (frag, crossbow bolt, RPG, SMG grenade and a shot barrel at a citizen keep its health; a Combine frag still hurts it). Not in this step: ai_relationship overrides, NPC bullets (HitSource has no attacker id), NPC blast damage feeding AI pain/damaged-by-player, metered NPC ball damage.

14n session 33 playtest fixes (owner playtest of session 32, 2026-10-27). (a) Dropped NPC weapons: CBaseCombatWeapon::FallInit puts them in COLLISION_GROUP_WEAPON, which CGameRules::ShouldCollide keeps off players, NPCs and vehicles (and debris off everything): new `WEAPON_GROUPS` (world, props, frags, other weapons; bullets, +USE and the gravity gun still hit them), excluded from player movement (Rapier filter and the convex SAT path) and NPC hull traces. Their collider was the bind mesh turned 90 degrees from the drawn idle pose because the .phy adapter required one identity root bone; PhysModelCreate uses a VPhysics prop's solid as stored at the entity's origin/angles, and an owned check shows the w_stunbaton/w_pistol/w_irifle solids match the idle pose (within 1 unit) and not the bind mesh, so `read_physics` accepts any MDL 44-49 root (`phy::studio_checksum`; static props keep the identity adapter). (b) Crossbow pins to doors: SDK and retail BoltTouch (server.dll body at 0x10411d10, no Ghidra function: private friendly-projectiles-20261027) dispatch BoltImpact behind a struck NPC only for the world or MOVETYPE_NONE entities; doors (CBaseProp::Spawn, CBasePropDoor::Spawn) are MOVETYPE_PUSH. Native A/B (oracle view-d1_trainstation_02-20261027T092202Z: a cop 40 units in front of the station hall's closed prop_door_rotating pair, crossbow from the corridor): the corpse falls to the floor, not pinned. Ours matches; no change until the owner names the door. (c) Shotgun punch: retail ammo forces equal ours (Buckshot 1308.7, pistol 668.0, 357 10905.7 kg in/s); native 60 fps recordings (private shotgun-punch-20261027) show the front-facing cop airborne 0.1 s after the shot and landing at ~0.35 s, while ours only slides (pelvis 130-160 in/s, at rest after 0.7 s). Missing: death poses. CAI_BaseNPC::Event_Killed -> SelectDeathPose (unless DMG_PREVENT_PHYSICS_FORCE; CNPC_Combine only when not crouching): GetDeathPoseActivity from -damage force against the NPC's forward/right (ACT_DIE_FRONTSIDE/BACKSIDE/LEFTSIDE/RIGHTSIDE), frame from LastHitGroup (head 1, stomach/chest 2, left arm 3, right arm 4, left leg 5, right leg 6; generic keeps the activity with frame 1), SelectWeightedSequence. Client C_AI_BaseNPC::GetRagdollInitBoneArrays: bones 0.1 s before death, the death pose sequence at cycle frame/6 (GetRagdollCurSequenceWithDeathPose) and the current bones; CRagdoll::Init applies the death force (RagdollCreate) and then RagdollApplyAnimationAsVelocity(prev, deathpose, 0.1): every solid gains its bone's (death pose - previous pose)/0.1 s linear and angular velocity. Bounded steps: load the ACT_DIE_* sequences with every NPC rig; record LastHitGroup per NPC in the hit path; in `ragdolls::spawn`, choose the pose and add the per-solid velocities (the pose 0.1 s earlier is taken as the current pose plus the existing root velocity; the moving-NPC origin extrapolation term is not ported). Validation: unit tests (activity/frame selection, velocities from a synthetic pose), packaged pk-shotgun/pk-pistol trajectories against the native recordings, regression batch. Result: the death poses lowered the shotgun push (police deathpose_front frame 2 folds the head forward: 31 units); parked on `wip/session33b-20261027` until a native check of the pose. Pushing the new ragdoll with the killing shot's impact effects reached the native range (114 units) but is withdrawn: retail CRagdollEnumerator::EnumElement FUN_1012ff00 and CRagdollExplosionEnumerator FUN_102434c0 skip ragdolls created on the current tick, as the SDK. Even with it, an SMG grenade kill moved half as far as native: the gap is the ragdoll's physics response (next step: a controlled impulse A/B). Pistol crumple slide (~30 vs ~12) open.

14n session 34 crossbow pins behind doors (owner case from session 33; branch `wip/session34-20261028`). BoltTouch's 128-unit trace behind a struck NPC is UTIL_TraceLine(MASK_BLOCKLOS) with the standard filter: StandardFilterRules (SDK util_shared.cpp; retail server.dll CTraceFilterSimple::ShouldHitEntity FUN_1027bf80 -> FUN_1027c0a0, identical) only hits brush models with SOLID_BSP/SOLID_VPHYSICS unless the mask has CONTENTS_MONSTER, skips transparent entities without CONTENTS_WINDOW and MOVETYPE_PUSH ones without CONTENTS_MOVEABLE. So the trace passes studio models (prop_door_rotating, props, NPCs) and pins against the world behind them; brush doors (MOVETYPE_PUSH) stop it and fail BoltTouch's MOVETYPE_NONE test. Steps: (1) `Physics::masked_ray` (world-brush contents mask; MASK_BLOCKLOS 0x4041 also lets world windows/grates through) and `weapon_crossbow::blocklos_filter` (world/static props and opaque brush entities). (2) The pin itself: `Physics::ragdoll_ray` tests every ragdoll solid directly instead of the query tree, which does not hold a ragdoll created this tick (the bolt's kill builds the ragdoll a tick after the impact; the client's BoltImpact already sees it). Validation: unit test (studio door skipped, brush door blocks), owned test on d1_trainstation_02 (the hall `noexit` doors at x -3464 sit 0.5 units in front of the world; the east `station_entrance` doors have nothing within 128 units: native no-pin T092202Z), packaged pin-hall fixture against native T104329Z, `bolt_misses`/`last_missed_bolt` diagnostics.
14n session 34 ragdoll punch (owner: shotgun kills lack native punch). Re-measurement first: session 33's native recordings held +attack for ~5 s (each oracle view costs ~2.8 s of screenshot overhead), so the weapon refired into the corpse (two shotgun flashes in ns1, a second 357 shot at +0.82 s), and its tracker (dark pixels, 75-degree horizontal FOV) locked onto walls and shadows. Single-shot native recordings (private ragdoll-punch-20261028/record_single.sh: one cfg line "+attack;wait 3;-attack") measured by background subtraction with the real projection (fov 75 keeps the 4:3 vertical FOV: 0.617 units/px at the floor) put our corpses at ~1.6x native (357 140 vs 85, shotgun 119 vs 69), not half; but those native cops stood sideways (native SetHealth self-damage turns them; ours do not turn), so front-facing runs without sethealth decide the A/B. Retail evidence for three ragdoll differences: (a) death poses (SDK death_pose.cpp, CAI_BaseNPC::SelectDeathPose, C_AI_BaseNPC::GetRagdollInitBoneArrays, RagdollApplyAnimationAsVelocity; ported from the parked wip/session33b-20261027 without its withdrawn killing-shot push); (b) C_ClientRagdoll::HandleAnimatedFriction: every client think while the model's animatedfriction runs (police: min 1, max 1000, in 0.8 s, hold 0, out 0.5 s) sets SetAngularMotor(0, m_iCurrentFriction) on every ragdoll constraint (m_iCurrentFriction starts 0, so models without it lose their joint friction at the first think); (c) torque units: retail vphysics.dll converts constraint torques x DEG2RAD (SetAngularMotor FUN_1000c820 -> FUN_10031810 for ragdolls, InitHinge FUN_1000b110 for phys_hinge; DAT_100acf58 = 0.0174533), not our x1000 x 0.0254^2 (ragdoll 0.2 friction was 37x strong, phys_hinge 27x weak: session 17's tic-tac-toe block). The ragdoll constraint init's own conversion is not read (assumed DEG2RAD). Steps: (1) death poses; (2) `Physics::ragdoll_joint_friction` + `AnimatedFriction` per ragdoll in `Ragdolls::update`; (3) DEG2RAD for .phy joint friction and phys_hinge. Validation: unit tests (death pose selection, friction ramp), packaged pk-*/pkf-* fixtures against the single-shot native recordings, regression batch. Step (4) found by the front-facing A/B: our pellet batches applied each pellet separately, so a 40 HP cop died on a later pellet and its ragdoll got only the remaining pellets' force (~25 units vs native 96-99); `Inventory::hit_batch` gives a target the batch kills every pellet's summed force on the last pellet's solid. Result (front-facing, 40 HP, one shot): 357 native 122.5 / ours 127 units; shotgun native 95.5-99.2 / ours 127-133 (one spread each); SMG grenade beyond the 245-unit top view in both. Step (5), from the hinge re-test: CPhysExplosion::Explode aims at BodyTarget, which props do not override (CBaseEntity::BodyTarget -> WorldSpaceCenter, the collision OBB center); ours aimed at the mass center, 2 units off the tic-tac-toe hinge axis (session 17). `Physics::bounds_center` (the body-space AABB of its colliders at the body pose) replaces it.
14n session 35 ragdoll rest (owner playtest of session 34: bullets and the crowbar no longer pushed corpses after death; native pushes three corpses minutes later; branch `wip/session35-20261028`). Evidence: FX_AffectRagdolls (SDK client/fx_impact.cpp; retail client.dll FUN_1012ffa0: `if (1 < m_iSimulatedRagdollCount) return 0`) refuses every shot while more than one ragdoll simulates; CRagdollLRURetirement::Update (retail FUN_101aa3a0) counts a ragdoll as simulating while its root IPhysicsObject is awake; CRagdoll::VPhysicsUpdate -> CheckSettleStationaryRagdoll (SDK client/ragdoll.cpp; retail FUN_101a5390: RAGDOLL_SLEEP_TOLERANCE 1.0 per axis per update, ragdoll_sleepaftertime 5.0, then PhysForceClearVelocity and Sleep on every element) and C_ClientRagdoll::ImpactTrace -> ResetRagdollSleepAfterTime. Cause: our ragdolls never slept. Rapier rests an island only when every solid stays under 0.5 rad/s for 2 s, and landed police corpses keep a jittering limb at up to ~0.9 rad/s while the root creeps ~1 unit/s, so from session 32 (when post-death pushes arrived with the native rule) any two corpses refused every later shot; session 34's DEG2RAD joint friction (37x weaker) adds limb jitter. A pre-34 build (a8d41f3) refused all 10 shots with two corpses too. Sleep is not a loss of interaction: a resting body still collides and any impulse wakes it (native IVP rests corpses moments after landing, so its refusal window is the tumble right after a multi-kill). Steps: (1) `ragdolls::Settle` (CheckSettleStationaryRagdoll per 15 ms update while any solid is awake; the native check runs per client frame, so its 1-unit tolerance is ~67 units/s here) and ResetRagdollSleepAfterTime on shot and blast pushes; `Physics::ragdoll_sleep`. (2) Ragdoll solids rest under 2 rad/s for 0.5 s (Rapier activation; linear stays 0.4 m/s, ~16 units/s); tuned to the hall corpses, IVP's deactivation constants are not read. (3) Diagnostics: `simulating`, `shots_busy`, `forced_sleeps`, per-solid `activity`. Validation: unit tests (settle timing and impact reset; a landed two-body ragdoll rests on its own, an impact wakes it, a forced sleep holds), packaged artifacts/s35 fixtures (make_fixtures.py: c-two-sleep, c-two-shots, c-pistol, c-crowbar; pre-34 comparisons *-pre34build), regression batch. Result: two corpses rest ~1 s after landing (3.2 s after the kill; before: never); with two corpses down 10 of 11 later pistol shots push (the refusal came while both still tumbled); single-corpse pistol and crowbar 8/8. Open: native time-to-rest A/B (oracle; it refuses while the owner's hl2.exe runs) and per-hit push size against native.

14n session 35b force bone and pellet batches (2026-10-29, branch `wip/session35-20261029` off main 304abfd). Plan: (1) CBaseEntity::TraceAttack adds multidamage only with m_takedamage, so world hits must not apply a target's pending pellets (SDK baseentity_shared.cpp); a unit test with a world pellet between a cop's pellets, failing on the old flush. (2) Shotgun overshoot (ours 127-133 vs native 95.5-99.2): first a native probe of how many pellets hit (sk_metropolice_health 100 so the cop survives, ent_dump health after one shell), then compare the force solid. Result of (2): native 100 -> 44 HP, all seven pellets hit; our nearest-origin force solid was the 2.5 kg left upper arm for an off-centre chest pellet (its share of the impulse exceeds the 2000 in/s limit, so the shotgun and the 357 saturated alike). CAI_BaseNPC::TraceAttack sets m_nForceBone = ptr->physicsbone (the hit hitbox bone's physics bone; studio physicsbone = the solid of the bone or its nearest simulated ancestor). Implementation: `RayHit.bone` (hitbox colliders remember their studio bone), `Scene::npc_force_bones` set per hit like npc_hit_groups, `ragdolls::physics_bone` at creation; the nearest-origin rule stays as the fallback for hits without a hitbox (melee hulls; blasts keep the root). Validation: unit tests, packaged front-facing fixtures (artifacts/s35/b-*), regression batch. Not done: a per-class m_takedamage table (brush entities), death-pose frame details, the pistol crumple slide.

Order inside OnTakeDamage_Alive after the base damage: threshold = min(1, damage / max health); ShouldReleaseHeadcrab (a lethal blast -> RELEASE_RAGDOLL: headless), then ShouldBecomeTorso (DMG_BLAST, threshold >= 0.5, not a torso, not slumped, not DMG_REMOVENORAGDOLL): CanBecomeLiveTorso (= has its head) -> BecomeTorso; a headless zombie instead dies chopped 1 time in 3 (legs + torso raggibs). Result (session 37): implemented as planned, plus (a) `ai_hull` (hull table, class hulls, WorldSpaceCenter/EyePosition heights: the AI's hard-coded 36/64 now follow the class for sight, claws and enemy targets); (b) the torso's crawl drifts 0.2 degrees sideways (0.13 of 37.4 units a cycle), so the locomotion check accepts lateral drift up to 1% of the direction and 0.5% of the cycle (the motor moves along the path at the ground speed); (c) TASK_FALL_TO_GROUND and a MOVETYPE_STEP gravity fall (600) that runs whether or not the AI thinks; (d) NPC hull traces skip client ragdolls in their convex path (the torso landed on its own legs gib; any corpse could block NPC probes; unit test fails without the fix); (e) raggib templates (`spawn::GIB_MODELS`, class `raggib`, sequence 0 loaded) and `Ragdolls::spawn_gib` (client ragdoll, entity marked dead like a corpse); (f) npc_zombie_torso in the class tables (relationships, health, head multipliers, blood). Evidence: owned tests (torso/legs sequences and eyes 24/0, crawl locomotion complete; hull grid: tiny and human hulls reach the same 20 of 20 hall targets); packaged artifacts/s37 torso-placed (crawls ~31 u/s to the player and claws), torso-blast (zombie 46 damage -> torso 25 HP falls, chases, a 50% ignition burns it to death after ~5.5 s, torso ragdoll), torso-burst-near (legs thrown down the corridor, torso on the floor); native oracle with the same layout (private research notes, native-torso-3: burning live torso on the floor; native-torso-2 with the grenade at the zombie: whole-body ragdoll plus a separate headcrab ragdoll, the lethal branch). Runtime bodygroup plan (step 0 of the throws): the loader already builds one mesh set per (model, skin, body) (`read_model_body`, `ModelInstance::body`, `entity_body` picks the spawn body), so SetBodygroup can swap variants instead of masking surfaces: (a) the simulation keeps `Scene` body overrides set through `models::set_bodygroup` on a BodyPart table cached per model at load; (b) the host sees a changed body, requests that (model, skin, body) variant from the asynchronous late-precache worker (no blocking reads in frame systems) and keeps drawing the old variant until it is ready; (c) the poison zombie needs at most 16 variants (3 nest bits x the hand crab), the zombie's headcrab group 2. Validation: unit tests on the body arithmetic, an owned test that the poison zombie's variants differ in surfaces, a packaged capture of the hand crab before a throw.
14o session 38 owner playtest fixes (2026-10-30, branch `wip/session38-20261030`). (1) Frame hitch at every zombie crab release (worse with live crabs): `Game::release_crab` held a second `Arc<World>` handle while it created the crab and the headless corpse, so `insert_entity`'s `Arc::make_mut` deep-copied the whole map World (report `runtime_entities.world_copies` 1 in every session 37 release run; `split_zombies` had the same pattern before its legs raggib). Fix: read the World through the field, no extra handle while entities are created. Measured with `--profile` (new `max_ms`/`slowest` per CPU stage) on artifacts/s38 crab-release-body: the kill tick 449 ms before, 13 ms max after, world copies 1 -> 0, no render-stage spike above 19 ms. (2) The player stuck after a headcrab leap: the airborne crab step (`AiRuntime::step_falls`) traced only the world and NPC colliders, then tested the player's box after moving, so the crab ended inside the player's box, landed at his feet and every player move started solid (CheckStuck's 2-unit nudges cannot clear a 24-unit crab). SDK physics_main.cpp PhysicsStepRunTimestep -> PhysicsTryMove traces the MOVETYPE_STEP crab with its solid mask, which the player's box blocks; on a hit it runs the touch (LeapTouch) and ClipVelocity (overbounce 1), and a floor-normal hit on a standable entity sets the ground entity. Ours: the player's box (36/72 high, none when dead or noclip) is a CONTENTS_MONSTER transient in the fall trace; a player hit is the LeapTouch contact (no more overlap test), the velocity is clipped like a wall (the crab slides down beside him), and a hit on his top face first rested the crab there without landing (replaced the same session: it now drops off his nearest side, see the throws entry below). Deviation for our tick order (the player moves after the NPCs against colliders posed before them, so a crab can start a tick a few units inside his box): a faller starting inside the player's box is first pushed out sideways (shallowest horizontal side that is clear, +DIST_EPSILON; `ai.diagnostics.player_box_pushouts`), else the player box is left out of that step. Fixture artifacts/s38 crab-stuck (crab leaps at the hall walk start, then the player strafes, walks forward and back): old build stuck from the third bite to the end with the crab inside his box; fixed build never stuck. (3) Poison recovery (owner, native: a poison bite cannot take the player below one health but lowers the health the recovery returns to; enough bites leave one health without regeneration): already the SDK model (CBlackHeadcrab::TouchDamage: DMG_SLASH sk_headcrab_melee_dmg, then DMG_POISON health - 1; only m_nPoisonDmg is restored), now `PlayerDamage::poison_bite` in hl2-simulation with a repeated-bite test (95, 90, ... 10, 5, then 1 for good). Found, not changed: a light prop_physics (d1_trainstation_02 `junk` cardboard box behind the hall start) rests ~0.1 units inside the player's box after he backs into it; alone CheckStuck frees him in ~0.3 s, but with a crab landed in front of him the nudges have nowhere to go. Source's player VPhysics shadow keeps props out of the box (finer physics, later).
14o session 38 runtime bodygroups and the poison zombie's crab throws (plan). Owned poison.mdl: body parts studio + headcrab1..5 (two submodels each, bases 1..16; group 1 the head crab, 2-4 the nest, 5 the crab in hand); `throw` ACT_RANGE_ATTACK2 (79 frames at 30 fps: AE_ZOMBIE_POISON_PICKUP_CRAB 0.29, THROW_SOUND 0.38, THROW_CRAB 0.5), `headcrab2leap` ACT_RANGE_ATTACK1 (81 frames: PICKUP 0.05, THROW 0.85), `throwwarning` ACT_ZOMBIE_POISON_THREAT (THROW_WARN_SOUND 0.08); attachments headcrab1..5. SDK npc_PoisonZombie.cpp: RangeAttack1Conditions (crabs left, m_flNextCrabThrowTime passed, 12..256, cone 0.6) and RangeAttack2Conditions (250..800, cone 0.6) pick RandomThrowCrab; PrescheduleThink sets the first throw 1-2 s after COND_NEW_ENEMY; OnTakeDamage_Alive of 20% max health or more allows a throw at once; TranslateSchedule: RANGE_ATTACK1/2 -> SCHED_ZOMBIE_POISON_RANGE_ATTACK1 (stop, face enemy, announce, TASK_RANGE_ATTACK1) / _ATTACK2 (stop, face ideal, the threat sequence facing the enemy, face ideal, TASK_RANGE_ATTACK2), MOVE_TO_WEAPON_RANGE -> CHASE_ENEMY, COMBAT_FACE when unreachable -> TAKE_COVER; SelectFailSchedule: CHASE_ENEMY_FAILED with crabs -> ESTABLISH_LINE_OF_FIRE; MustCloseToAttack only without crabs; selection is CNPC_BaseZombie's then CAI_BaseNPC::SelectCombatSchedule (COND_TOO_CLOSE_TO_ATTACK from the throw check backs away first; SetSchedule clears conditions like ours). Events: PICKUP_CRAB EnableCrab(off) + SetBodygroup(5, 1); THROW_CRAB SetBodygroup(5, 0), a npc_headcrab_poison (GetHeadcrabClassname; the same CBlackHeadcrab as npc_headcrab_black) at EyePosition owned by the zombie (no collision with it), SF_NPC_FALL_TO_GROUND (+ fade corpse), ACT_RANGE_ATTACK1, ThrowAt(enemy eyes) = JumpAttack thrown (height clamp 16..400, speed cap 1000), next throw U{4..10} s, no crabs -> no range capabilities. Event_Killed (not a torso): EvacuateNest (the crab in hand rejoins; each nest crab at headcrab2/3/4 with a random yaw, HeadcrabFits, Eject at velocity scale 2, or 0.1 x damage for blasts over 10: RandomInt(20, 50) x (U(-1,1), U(-1,1), U(0.5,1)), enemy = attacker, alert, EjectTouch = LeapTouch then Panic 2-8 s on landing). Steps: (1) runtime bodygroups: World.model_body_parts (nummodels, base per part) for models of classes whose AI changes bodygroups (npc_poisonzombie), and their 16 nest x hand variants preloaded in model_assets at map load (no reads in frame systems); Scene.bodies holds a changed body per entity (`set_bodygroup`), the host's swap system despawns that entity's draws and rebuilds them from the variant's surfaces with the base key's rig, lighting and animation (entities with eye or bone-merged weapon draws are refused and reported); (2) the throw AI above in ai::zombie (Kind::Poison) and the runtime (conditions, events, a `crab_throws` request the host turns into a black crab from its pool template, thrown through the shared fall with the zombie excluded from its trace until it lands); (3) EvacuateNest/Eject on death; panic hops later. Validation: unit tests (variant arithmetic, conditions, schedule translation, throw velocity), an owned test that the 16 variants load with different surface counts, a packaged d1_town_02 fixture (a poison zombie 400 units from the walking player: threat, pickup with the crab in hand, the black crab flies at the player and the nest loses one crab), then a native oracle A/B of the same layout.
14o session 39 idle hiccups (owner playtest 2026-10-30, branch `wip/session39-20261030`: NPCs jerk every few seconds, e.g. the d1_trainstation_02 can cop `cupcop` sharply turns and resets his head; native does not). Evidence: fixture artifacts/s39 cupcop-look (21 s watching the cop, a sample every 2 ticks; head_summ.py): head goals change smoothly (no step over 3 degrees per sample), but `batonidle1` (police_animations.mdl: ACT_IDLE weight 2, looping, 301 frames at 30 fps = 10 s, fade in/out 0.2; batonidle2 weight 1, 6.7 s) restarted at frame 0 every 5.04 s (4.08, 9.12, 14.16, 19.2 s): SCHED_IDLE_STAND is TASK_STOP_MOVING, TASK_SET_ACTIVITY ACT_IDLE, TASK_WAIT 5, TASK_WAIT_PVS, and our TASK_SET_ACTIVITY always restarted the activity. SDK: TASK_SET_ACTIVITY is SetIdealActivity (ai_basenpc_schedule.cpp), which re-resolves the ideal sequence through SelectWeightedSequence(activity, current) (animation.cpp: the current sequence is kept only when its weight is negative, else a weighted random pick); MaintainActivity (ai_basenpc.cpp) changes nothing while activity and sequence equal the ideal ones, and SetActivityAndSequence resets the cycle only for a different (or non-looping) sequence; SetActivity also returns at once for the current activity. Sequence changes then blend on the client: C_BaseAnimating::MaintainSequenceTransitions / CSequenceTransitioner (sequence_Transitioner.cpp) keeps the previous sequence playing at its own rate and accumulates it over the new one with GetFadeout's weight (1 - elapsed / min(previous fadeouttime, new fadeintime), on the 3s^2 - 2s^3 spline); STUDIO_SNAP or no-interpolation clears the queue; a restart of the same sequence (new-sequence parity) also blends. We had no base-sequence blending at all, so every idle/walk/gesture switch popped. Steps: (1) `Motor::reselect_activity` for TASK_SET_ACTIVITY of the running activity (SceneMotor: the same translation, `Scene::ai_reselect_activity` = weighted pick with the current sequence, a switch only to another sequence); other set_activity callers keep their restart (they port SetActivity/ResetIdealActivity uses such as attacks). (2) `Scene::update_sequence_transitions` after each host gameplay tick (after the AI): per npc_ actor, a changed sequence name or a sequence time going back pushes the previous sequence (its time continues) with fade min(fade_out, fade_in); `GestureLayers::compose_blended` accumulates the fades newest first between the base and the gesture layers; `pose_signature` includes them so the host's pose cache separates fading actors; report `sequence_fades`. Result: cupcop-look after the fix plays batonidle1 continuously (sequence time 15.7 s at 19.8 s); the map-start walk -> idle -> itemhit changes and the one restart when the AI takes over from the script at 4.07 s now fade over 0.2 s. Not done: scripted/scene sequence changes outside npc_ classes (props, cameras) do not blend; the AI-takeover restart itself (SDK m_Activity after a script is not checked); a native side-by-side capture of the cop.
14o session 39 poison zombie goes passive after a throw (owner playtest 2026-10-30: after one thrown crab it froze idle, or walked up, then away, and would not attack while he stood in front of it). Reproduced: artifacts/s39 poison-close (d1_town_02 start corridor, the session 38 throw layout; the player walks up after the first throw): when the next throw came due at 8.72 s with the player ~100 units away, the zombie looped SCHED_BACK_AWAY_FROM_ENEMY -> TASK_FIND_BACKAWAY_FROM_SAVEPOSITION fails -> SCHED_FAIL, interrupted every think by COND_CAN_RANGE_ATTACK1, until the end of the run (two schedule changes per think; with room to back off it walked away instead). Cause: RangeAttack1Conditions (the crab leap, 12..256) gave COND_CAN_RANGE_ATTACK1 while RangeAttack2Conditions (the throw, 250..800) gave COND_TOO_CLOSE_TO_ATTACK, and CAI_BaseNPC::SelectCombatSchedule checks TOO_CLOSE first. SDK CAI_BaseNPC::GatherAttackConditions ends by clearing COND_TOO_CLOSE_TO_ATTACK, COND_TOO_FAR_TO_ATTACK and COND_WEAPON_BLOCKED_BY_FRIEND whenever any CAN_RANGE/MELEE condition is set; ours never did (all NPC classes). Fix: `conditions::clear_range_complaints` at the end of the visible-enemy attack gather. poison-close after the fix: at 9.0 s the zombie leaps a crab off its back (SCHED_ZOMBIE_POISON_RANGE_ATTACK1), then chases and claws (the player dies at 12.6 s).

14o session 39 script-to-AI activity: SDK scripted.cpp StartSequence calls SetActivityAndSequence(GetSequenceActivity(seq) or ACT_IDLE, seq, ...), so m_Activity follows the script's sequence and a following TASK_SET_ACTIVITY of that activity only re-rolls. 14o session 40 owner playtest plan (2026-10-31, branch `wip/session40-20261031`). Reports: thrown gas cans/propane never ignite NPCs (only blast-split torsos burn); zombies lack their burning scream/animation; sawblades neither cut zombies nor kill NPCs and turn freely in the gravity gun; poison zombie blast deaths leave all nest crabs alive (native: mostly one); panicking crabs merge into one spot; the crosshair turns white with the gravity gun. SDK evidence and steps, each validated separately:
(1) Crosshair: the HUD's weapon-script list omitted weapon_physcannon and weapon_bugbait (both TextureData crosshair: Crosshairs font "Q"), so they fell back to the white default sprite. Add them. Validation: hl2-ui test, packaged capture with the gravity gun.
(2) Gas-can ignition: CBreakableProp::Break with PROPINTER_PHYSGUN_BREAK_EXPLODE ("onbreak" "explode_fire", the gas can and propane tank) ignites every combat character in a CEntitySphereQuery of m_explodeRadius around the break origin that passes the damage filter (Ignite(30), bNPCOnly true, not by a designer: only AllowedToIgnite classes: CNPC_BaseZombie, CNPC_CombineS, CBaseHeadcrab, CNPC_MetroPolice; never the player or citizens). `Scene::ignite_npc` gathers the NPC ignition rules (designer inputs ignore AllowedToIgnite; CNPC_BaseZombie::Ignite caps health to 5 x (10 +- 2)); the bursts carry their radius and attacker to the ignition after ExplosionCreate.
(3) Burning zombies: CNPC_BaseZombie::Ignite switches ACT_WALK/RUN/IDLE to the _ON_FIRE activity at once when the model has it (not in act-busy); CZombie::Ignite (classic and torso, alive, not low violence) ungags and starts its moan patch (NPC_BaseZombie.Moan1..4 by m_iMoanSound = spawn order mod 4, CHAN_STATIC, Play(1, 100)) with envZombieMoanIgnited ({1, U(0.5,1)}, {1, 30}, {0, U(0.5,1)}) and pitch 120 over 1 s (SoundChangeVolume(1, 1) is a no-op at volume 1); CZombie::Extinguish fades it (0 and pitch 100 over 2 s); StopLoopingSounds destroys it at death; burning zombies make no pain sounds (CZombie::PainSound) and CanPlayMoanSound is false while burning. Burning cops: COND_METROPOLICE_ON_FIRE -> METROPOLICE_ON_FIRE sentence and SCHED_METROPOLICE_BURNING_STAND (ACT_IDLE_ON_FIRE, wait 1.5, TASK_METROPOLICE_DIE_INSTANTLY DMG_BURN, wait 1), no pain/death sentences while burning, ACT_RUN -> ACT_RUN_ON_FIRE. Burning Combine soldiers (when their AI exists): SCHED_COMBINE_BURNING_STAND. The transition is detected per tick from Fires so every ignition path (blast torso, burn damage, inputs, gas cans) gets it.
(4) Sawblades: CPhysicsProp spawn gives spin_zaxis props (sawblade001a) FVPHYSICS_DMG_SLICE; CalculatePhysicsImpactDamage then adds the angular path for slice objects: ReadDamageTable(npcAngularTable {100^2: 10, 150^2: 25, 200^2: 50, 250^2: 500}, DotProductAbs(inertia, local angular velocity in deg/s) x energyScale) above 360 deg/s, with DMG_SLASH added to DMG_CRUSH; CNPC_BaseZombie::IsChopped (lethal, >= half max health, SLASH and CRUSH) -> DieChopped with "E3_Phystown.Slicer". OnPhysGunDrop LAUNCHED_BY_CANNON adds a 5000 deg/s local z spin and clears the angular drag; Pickup_GetPreferredCarryAngles holds spin_zaxis props at m_preferredCarryAngles (-5, 0, 0) in the player's entity frame (pitch and yaw), and a model's physgun_interactions preferred_carryangles holds it in ComputePlayerMatrix's frame (pitch 0). Units of VPhysics GetInertia to be confirmed (retail vphysics.dll) before trusting the angular energies.
(5) Nest crabs: CNPC_BaseZombie::HeadcrabFits traces a tiny hull 1 unit down ignoring only the new crab and the zombie, so crabs ejected earlier in EvacuateNest block later nest spots that overlap them (the removed crab is dropped). Ours runs the trace before the new crabs are solid; the evacuation must test against the crabs it already created. Measure the poison.mdl headcrab2/3/4 attachment spacing first.
(6) Crabs merging: native crabs do not collide with each other (HL2COLLISION_GROUP_HEADCRAB) but CAI_PlaneSolver::GenerateObstacleNpcs (ProbeForNpcs() is 0) steers every mover around other live NPCs and the player as soft obstacles, and CAI_TacticalServices::FindCoverNode locks the chosen node for 1 s. Port the cover lock first (shared), then a bounded steering step for NPC-NPC avoidance; validate with the poison-kill nest fixture (crab separations over time).
Result (session 40, packaged on d1_trainstation_02's hall, artifacts/s40 make_fixtures.py/run.sh/summ.py): (1) gravgun-crosshair: the HUD's yellow circle (owned hl2-ui test: weapon_physcannon and weapon_bugbait read "Q" and draw in the crosshair color). (2) gascan-ignite/gascan-burning: pistol shots break a gas can 72 units from a zombie's hull; PropaneTank.Burst, the zombie is ignited (report npc_ignitions), its health capped to 42 and burned down 1 per 0.15 s, dead at 12 s; Scene::ignite_near/ignite_npc, unit test (only AllowedToIgnite classes within the radius; citizens refused; designer inputs burn anyone). (3) The burning moan (NPC_BaseZombie.Moan1..4 by spawn order, Play 1/100, envZombieMoanIgnited, pitch 120) starts with the AI; the burning walk needed its own locomotion variant (the controller plays locomotion sequences itself): `firewalk` (ACT_WALK_ON_FIRE) drifts 0.67 degrees and 0.0024 units short of its mean speed, so validate_forward_track's drift tolerances widened (direction 0.015, lateral 1.5% of the stride, along-track 1e-4 of the stride); gascan-burning shows firewalk with flames. Combine soldiers' burning stand not done. (4) Inertia units: the retail vphysics.dll has no inches^2 constant (float/double 1550.0031 or 0.00064516 absent) and no squared use of its 39.37/0.0254 globals (lab s40-units-xref.txt; the CPhysicsObject vtable route found no symbol), so GetInertia is taken as kg m^2 (Rapier's own unit). saw-launch: the held blade launched at the zombie's torso: E3_Phystown.Slicer, 110 damage, DieChopped (legs, torso thrown up, a live crab this roll); saw-hold: the blade held level in front of the view; owned prop data: sawblade001a spin_z and world_stick, harpoon002a preferred_carryangles 0 0 0. (4b, owner follow-up: blades nail into walls) CBreakableProp::HandleFirstCollisionInteractions -> HandleInteractionStick for onworldimpact stick props: the first collision after a gravity gun launch, against the world, over 1000 u/s, dot(velocity, normal away) > 0.3, not into metal/grate/'X' surfaces: StickAtPosition after the step (origin + 8 along the velocity, motion disabled, debris, Metal.SawbladeStick); a gravity gun or +USE pickup (Physgun_OnPhysGunPickup, SF_PHYSPROP_ENABLE_ON_PHYSCANNON) frees it. impact_damage::world_sticks (unit test), Physics::stick, Impacts::collision_game_material; saw-wall: the blade embedded level in the hall's stone pillar. (5) poison-blast and poison-kill reruns: one nest crab ejects, the other two "do not fit" (both runs; before: three). (6) Cover node lock done (unit test: a locked node is not expanded, as in the SDK). Steering correction: CAI_LocalNavigator::MoveCalcRaw steers only when the direct move is blocked or explicit obstacles exist (AddObstacle: companions' danger sounds, the antlion guard), and MoveLimit's CTraceFilterNav uses the mover's own collision group, so a crab's direct move never hits another crab (HL2COLLISION_GROUP_HEADCRAB): native crabs do not steer around each other either; they stay apart by the one-crab nest rule, random eject directions and the node lock. No plane solver port for this report. (7) Native A/B of the ignition (hl2_complete oracle, the same hall layout: npc_create npc_zombie, prop_physics_create the gas can, ent_fire !picker break, notarget lifted; private torso-split-20261029/record_gascan.py, native-gascan-1.mkv, gascan1-sheet.png, session view-d1_trainstation_02-20261031T173843Z): the break ignites the zombie ("npc_zombie died to entityflame"); it burns ~11 s (ours ~9 s; both inside the 40-60 health cap at 5/s), standing in its burning idle and turning black; its ragdoll burns ~6 s more. Ours lacked the blackening: CNPC_BaseZombie::OnTakeDamage_Alive's Scorch(ZOMBIE_SCORCH_RATE 8, ZOMBIE_MIN_RENDERCOLOR 50) on each accepted burn hit, now `Scene::render_colors` (runtime SetRenderColor, from the rendercolor keyvalue) and the host's present_render_colors (the entity's and its client ragdoll's materials; unit test).
